| PowerShell Expand-Archive is broken in agent sessions — extract zips with Node + JSZip or tar | release |
| Reconstruction priority ladder: memory cache → IndexedDB → inline JSON → additive synthesis from seed → pattern synthesis | database |
| Stub tests (toBeDefined-only) count as no tests — every public function needs real unit assertions before release | release gates |
| The audio reconstruction priority chain: IndexedDB hex stream first (bonus), convergentSynthesis second (main, works without IndexedDB) with 72 plus cross-restriction parameters and the truth funnel of ZCR, flatness, centroid, skewness, kurtosis and checksum | streaming |
| the fake parts of the reference are never repeated: ~200 decorative *Id/*Hash fields per second, the expandFromSeed checksum claim, hex semantic signatures (0xKICK_SUB_HEAVY), empty scaffolds | audio json |
| The lyric ghostwriter law: words of at most eight characters, lowercase tags (intro, verse, chorus, outro) with no numbers, one unified lyric per zip folding every version into A, the final fused lyric at ninety-eight percent probability, never reuse a word or structure from a previous version, banned words (no cap, soft, robotic rhyme), never insert titles or metadata into the lyric body | writing style |

## cadria (12)

the image, video and 3d studio: server-side raster, the versawaze engine and the platform flow.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| Cadria raster runs by software on the server; the gpu of the visitor is never used | doctrine |
| Deleted apps are forbidden from returning (create, iukka) | doctrine |
| FFmpeg is banned inside the apps; webcodecs is the replacement | doctrine |
| Never ship a fake quality selector: implement real track switching or remove the menu entirely | doctrine |
| cadria is video and image player/editor/studio; absorbs iukka and create; houses versawaze engine at root | tree law |
| Competitor clones live flat inside allan/docs/temp (no nesting, no .git) and get pushed | tree law |
| Player extensibility verdict: features must arrive as a plugin system (Clappr's 6 plugin types as gold standard), never by modifying the monolithic core | qz plugin |
| The absorbed iukka rides the universal media player; the featuresupdate hierarchy is the mandatory change log of features | app roster |
| The render never touches the visitor gpu: the software raster rides the server side over https and the browser only displays the returned frames | 3d pipeline |
| Cadria owns the 3D/image/video/animation engine (VersaWaze); stealthhead depends on it via library and keeps only storage/compilation | stealth |
| repositories.md is two concatenated catalogs (988 github repos in 36 categories plus 21,818 npm packages in 40 categories); the clone-now list rides a 3 gb budget and a credential-pattern scan must return zero tokens | secrets |
| Never damage the working version's logic: only refine and add; never touch dependency manifests or the large-file upload process | version lockstep |

## stealthhead (504)

the fps and games application: precompiled assets, the database as vgpu, the 3d pipeline and the ten-step validation.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| One-way platforms collide only when falling and arriving from above (previous bottom above platform top); Down drops through | doctrine |
| A PMREM-filtered environment is mandatory for any PBR validation | doctrine |
| A readable screenshot is a hard prerequisite for visual implementation claims; inline previews alone are not evidence | browser automation |
| A vision read below confidence 0.55 is still used but flagged, never silently dropped; conflicts are written to the unknowns list, never silently overwritten | doctrine |
| Adjacent parts must physically connect by overlapping at their shared seam; parts that are merely near each other read as disconnected | doctrine |
| ADR-010: WebAudio is 100% synthesized (HRTF, resume on first gesture) | audio engine · ADR-010 |
| Aggressive dedup of doc trees: hash every file, keep exactly 1 per identical group, delete the rest (StealHead docs: 1640 scanned, 381 deleted, 10.28MB freed) | dedup law |
| Alt-Tab auto-pauses (tension audio ducks, menu shows); empty-mag auto-reload runs on an in-loop timer without drift | audio engine |
| Answers are always in PT-BR | doctrine |
| Any PR adding a new top-level file must justify it: an unowned domain, under 80KB, no cycle, compliant name, one coherent correlato group — otherwise absorb into an existing file | tree law |
| Audio: decode once into AudioBuffer during loading (decode-at-play = hitch); volume ramps via setTargetAtTime 10–50ms, never raw gain jumps; latencyHint 'interactive'; never setTimeout for rhythm timing | audio engine |
| Audio decodes once into AudioBuffers during the loading screen; never <audio> elements; gain ramps via setTargetAtTime | audio engine |
| Be transparent, never over-claim: state what changed with evidence, name what still does not match; never claim done when only improved; passing gate is not proof of 3d realism | doctrine |
| Camera and movement share the same forward vector; meshes look +Z and cameras −Z; movement uses yaw, never pitch; pitch clamps at ±89° | doctrine |
| Camera follows with a deadzone plus look-ahead, clamps to level bounds, and rounds to whole pixels for pixel art | doctrine |
| Camo repaints only _camo materials on receiver and magazine; tritium inserts are never tinted | doctrine |
| cannon-es Heightfield indexes matrix[row][col] as [z][x] — a transposed matrix rotates the terrain 90 degrees | physics |
| "Cannot reach the requested fidelity with this image" is a valid expected result — never fake confidence; hidden sides are inferred by mirroring with per-region confidence | doctrine |
| CapsuleGeometry weld trap: mergeVertices on the raw capsule generates 66 non-manifold artifacts — dedupe positionally and drop degenerate triangles (buildWatertightCapsule with own grid) | dedup law |
| Collision resolution: move/resolve X then Y separately, push out along the least-penetration axis and zero the velocity on the resolved axis | physics |
| Commits only on explicit request | doctrine |
| Comparison scripts only align and package evidence — the agent's vision inspects the sheet, never an automatic acceptance score | doctrine |
| Confirm the character stylization level before writing a spec (realistic ~7.5 heads / stylized 5-6 / chibi 2-3); never assume realistic by default | doctrine |
| CS2 items default to ultra-complex with a targetMinDetails floor of 9 — the skin is the point of the item | doctrine |
| Damage through walls is killed by multi-cell grid-hash collision (4m cells with DDA) — never single-cell walls | physics |
| Decoder paths always end with a trailing slash; one central getLoaders (DRACO+KTX2+Meshopt) serves every load | 3d pipeline |
| Default to Howler.js for generated games and wire the master + music + sfx gain buses from the first commit; map volume sliders through an x² curve; pitch-randomize repeated SFX ±10% and ship ogg/opus with mp3 fallbacks | doctrine |
| docs/ is never deleted — reference only, old code is never recycled | doctrine |
| Endless-runner spawners must always leave a reachable gap for the current speed; TD placement is validated by A* reachability before commit | doctrine |
| Forge scripts are pure Python 3.10+ stdlib — no pip install, no PIL/numpy, no Playwright | app roster |
| game prompts are one dense continuous paragraph, direct and objective, never bullet lists | doctrine |
| game prompts must name the exact weapon (AK-47) so the coder can code it; never define weapon geometry when realism is wanted | doctrine |
| Gaussian splats never replace gameplay meshes — splat only as backdrop or thumbnail; mesh + PBR keeps collision, LOD and deformation | 3d pipeline |
| Generated photos are never used as 3D meshes; image tools are 2D-only; same character/object across images is edit-chained from the existing base, never regenerated fresh | doctrine |
| Generative-assist meshes are flagged meshSource and never the silent default | doctrine |
| Gloves never reach 0.00 float — the cap is ~0.06–0.80; wear concentrates on knuckle guards, fingertips, palm/cuff edges and seams | doctrine |
| gltf-transform post-processing is mandatory: weld 0.0001, center pivot, dedup, prune, quantize position 14 bits (Class-A curves without faceting); never non-uniform scale; chassis CANNON.Box from Box3.getSize()/2 | dedup law |
| Gravity is −9.82, never −9.81 | physics |
| Host entrypoints are symlinks to one checkout, never independent copies (hosts drift apart silently) | cli craft |
| Human skin keeps metalness zero; painted metal is dielectric even over a metal substrate | doctrine |
| Implicit SDF path: ≤64 primitives, ≤128 operations, resolution 4–64; sdf and visualHill cannot coexist; sdf+subdivide not combinable; default specs stay byte-identical | doctrine |
| In-world reference mockups show at most 9 distinct candidates with no annotated diagrams (circles/arrows/outlines/labels are forbidden); a mockup is planning art — never a deliverable and never collision | physics |
| Input reads event.code (not key/keyCode) with Set-tracked held state read in the loop; clear the key Set on blur/visibilitychange | doctrine |
| Joint semantic aliases resolve only through an explicit map (mixamo:LeftArm → local joint id); never infer a joint from a string suffix | doctrine |
| Juice (sound, particles, hitstop, flash, shake) never alters gameplay | doctrine |
| Kill hitstop (30-120 ms, white flash) is presentation only and never changes the outcome; scores validate on the server — never trust the client | doctrine |
| Loading bars weight stages with a 0.9 cap for unfinished stages — never bytes-only | doctrine |
| LOD is mandatory (4 levels 30k/10k/3k/0.5k with distance bands); above 500 instances use InstancedMesh2 or BatchedMesh+BVH, never vanilla InstancedMesh without BVH | 3d pipeline |
| Mark a non-applicable step skipped only with a reason; silent omission forbidden; never bypass the local state gate by reconstructing progress from chat history | operation ladder |
| Match framing to the reference (object at ~98% of reference width); never rely on hard gates when framing intentionally differs | doctrine |
| Material analysis is an admission gate: every critical visible region needs hypothesis evidence before the build pass | doctrine |
| Mechanical pose probes (neutral rest, elbow/shoulder, hip/knee, end-effector reach, head/spine, hair attachment, ±35°/profile/rear views) pass before any likeness scoring or confidence claims | doctrine |
| Micro topology lives in shader nodes (one triplanar graph serves all); macro topology lives in the geometry kit (one kit builds the whole arena) | doctrine |
| Mixamo download uses the real logged-in browser with global node_modules, saving to docs/STEALHEAD | doctrine |
| Never bake lighting into the albedo map; never assign sRGB to mathematical data maps | doctrine |
| Never let a script score visuals — that is the agent's job; MCP-only scene mutations never count as implementation | doctrine |
| Never raw devicePixelRatio: setPixelRatio(Math.min(dpr, 1.5–2)) | 3d pipeline |
| Never ship a symmetric-parabola jump — fall gravity runs 1.5–2.5× rise gravity with apex hang and clamped terminal velocity | physics |
| Never take cap normals from the triangulation (ear-clipping returns slivers); the roll is per-vertex computed off the local half-width | doctrine |
| Never use JPEG for heightmap sources (ringing) and avoid 8-bit PNG (256-level stepping) — use Float32 or 16-bit raw | operation ladder |
| Never use JPEG heightmaps or non-uniform mesh scale (never mesh.scale.y); never rasterize terrain to a deformed mesh | doctrine |
| Never use Math.random(); use the shared ctx.rng | doctrine |
| Never use trimesh colliders for dynamic bodies — derive the collider from Box3/2 (cuboid); never apply non-uniform scale; 1 unit = 1 meter | physics |
| No extracted Valve texture may be committed or used to imply an exact-texture result | doctrine |
| Nothing has a 90-degree edge: every edge carries a chamfer | doctrine |
| Pack AO, roughness and metalness into one ORM texture (R/G/B channels) to cut texture bandwidth by two thirds | doctrine |
| Patterned CS2 skins ride de-lit reference projection; never hallucinate procedural noise for complex skins | doctrine |
| Presentation never changes simulation: juice is client-only and gameplay outcomes stay netcode-safe | doctrine |
| PWA for FPS uses display:fullscreen; the service worker is no longer required for installability but stays mandatory for offline-first | extension surface |
| Radical transparency: never say "done" when it is only "improved"; list what changed with exact values/coordinates and name what still does not match; a passing 2D gate proves no 3D realism | doctrine |
| Reconstructions define an attachment contract per child part (parent, socket, localStart, localEnd, contactType, gapTolerance, evidenceRefs); the pivot sits at localStart and parts never float; missing attachment lowers fidelity or requests input, never silent guessing | cli craft |
| Refine limits are safety limits, not targets: 3 refine decisions per pass, 6 total — stop earlier on success or plateau | doctrine |
| Regeneration is action-aware: a refine step never overwrites the artifact it must repair | operation ladder |
| Score at most five critical semantic features per pass (subsystems like hull/cabin/rig, not individual meshes) | doctrine |
| Scores are always validated server-side; client high scores are never trusted | doctrine |
| Scripts enforce and package; the agent's vision judges — visual scoring never moves into scripts | doctrine |
| Sequential ownership beats parallel fan-out for coupled systems (tonemapping, sky, GI) | doctrine |
| Subagents 0–300 with concurrency care (max 2 parallel, ~5s interval; real proved limit 1) | wave method |
| Terrain uses one shared Float32 height matrix for both visual mesh and CANNON.Heightfield so collision never diverges | physics |
| The opencode process is untouchable | qz plugin |
| The owner may spin up 0-300 subagents for a reading/analysis task | wave method |
| Tilesets demand a real 2×2 repeat composite check (PIL throwaway file) before delivery; cells painted edge-to-edge, never sticker tiles with gaps | tree law |
| Tuning canon: gravity -20.6, jump 4.97, sprint 7.01, coyote 0.09, head hitbox ×4.0 | physics |
| Uploaded front and back reference images strictly override preview fixtures and legacy code | doctrine |
| Vehicle yaw basis: A must increase yaw; flight A = bank left; camera and movement must agree; never alias a shared temp vector between camera and movement code | doctrine |
| Visual hull reconstruction marks hidden regions as low confidence instead of inventing detail | doctrine |
| Watertight primitives are built by construction (shared poles, mod radial) rather than by weld | doctrine |
| Weapon audio: round robin of 6 timbre slots per weapon (±1.1 semitone body, ±1.7 crack) — two consecutive shots never share a waveform | audio engine |
| weapon geometry is validated against the anatomy docs: bullpup = magazine behind the trigger; AK = banana magazine + visible gas tube + arched top cover; USP-S/AWP = suppressor/scope as identity | doctrine |
| Weapons are 100% geometric code (Box/Cylinder/glTF) — never photo/JPG sprites | doctrine |
| Weapons are generated from primitive batches merged per material (1 mesh per material) with muzzle/pivot points — never a 2D photo | doctrine |
| Web Audio uses buffers with setTargetAtTime — never gain.value and never <audio> for SFX | audio engine |
| Weld tolerance is 0.0001 (0.1 mm) — never 0.001, which glues doors to the chassis | doctrine |
| Zoom cannot create reference information — below pixel resolution the review returns insufficient-reference-resolution; super-resolution is never ground truth | doctrine |
| 3D player movement uses a Rapier KinematicCharacterController with capsule, autostep, snap-to-ground and slope limits — never a raw dynamic body | windows grammar |
| A* heuristics must be admissible and movement-matched (Manhattan for 4-dir, octile for 8-dir); binary-heap open set (linear scan is the #1 perf bug); flow fields when many agents chase one goal; AI runs on a fixed lower-rate timestep; FSM past ~6-8 states becomes a behavior tree | operation ladder |
| A local tools subfolder is always in .gitignore | tree law |
| A playable map is never one baked image: foundation without interactive props, props/collision as separate layers; reference mockups are checkpoints, never deliverables | physics |
| ADR-005: each domain is one file holding all its correlated logics; never split by responsibility; above ~80 KB move a correlate to a sibling file (~300 correlated logics per file) | tree law · ADR-005 |
| Anti-tunneling ships by default: swept AABB/CCD/raycast for fast objects, and a broadphase (spatial hash/quadtree) once past ~100 dynamic colliders | physics |
| Controls skill is mandatory before any WASD/vehicle/flight work — inverted A/D is the #1 ship-blocker | structure |
| Correction is localized: the loop decides at the (view, component, feature) cell and regenerates only the failing part's module | structure |
| CS2 family boundary is knife only: unsupported families and subtypes stop with unsupported-family, they never receive the knife component tree as a generic fallback | tree law |
| CS2 family limit: only knives and Glock-18 carry family contracts; everything else stops with unsupported-family — never receives another family's tree as generic fallback | tree law |
| Default to fixed-path (waypoint) tower defense with no runtime pathfinding; in maze TD a placement that fully blocks the path is rejected by an A* reachability check before commit | structure |
| Distribution builds without a dist folder, output straight to the root (SoFlowX pattern) | tree law |
| Dormant packages stay installed but never imported: rapier3d-compat + yuka (own physics), postprocessing (peer three<0.184), @react-three/drei + fiber (R3F vetoed — three vanilla at root), eslint cluster (lint is biome) | quality gates |
| Download order: main character first, then catalog, then profiles; final phase of the operation; scrape runs while game files are written | model catalog |
| Duplicated basenames ("(2)", "(3)") are always renamed by context when mirroring — noise (2).js ≠ noise.js | structure |
| Enemies never import combat — they listen via bus; no barrels, no central types.ts, no domain subfolders | tree law |
| Enemy spawn happens at arena edges/off-screen, never on the player; concurrency is capped | structure |
| engine.ts is the dependency center; state.ts is a leaf (only node:* imports); types live next to their consumer — never in types.ts; no index.ts re-export barrels | dependencies |
| Everything lowercase, no hyphen/underscore in names; zero barrels/re-exports | writing style |
| FPS rules: never render a weapon as a flat generated photo — build viewmodel from primitives/glTF parented to the camera in a separate overlay pass; pointer lock gesture-gated, read movementX/Y (never clientX/Y), clamp pitch ±89°, yaw the body and pitch the camera, movement uses yaw only; default hitscan raycast from screen center gated by cooldown with recoil+spread; ship juice (muzzle flash, hitmarker, hit sound, shake), minimal scope; mobile touch fallback | model catalog |
| Game-skill file compliance: lowercase, no hyphen/underscore, JSDoc in English, max 20 logics per file, node:* first, library-first, random host port, no vendor lock | writing style |
| Generated filenames are always new with timestamp plus random, markdown only in /mnt/data/final, never HTML | structure |
| Generated zip names are never reused: every zip carries a unique name with a random suffix verified against the existing list | structure |
| GLB-only at runtime; Draco qp 14/10/12; Meshopt quantize 14; KTX2 ETC1S q200 / UASTC level2 zstd18; LODs 30k/10k/3k lockBorder | 3d pipeline |
| gltf-transform pipeline: weld 0.0001, dedup, prune, quantize 14 bits, draco only after quantize; LOD 30k/10k/3k with lockBorder | dedup law |
| Golden rule: the root computes, /web only draws — the HUD reads state through onState listeners with per-frame snapshot copies, never internal game variables | 3d pipeline |
| Headless gltf pipeline order is fixed: center, instance, dedup, weld, prune, reorder, quantize, simplify LODs with lockBorder, then draco/meshopt and KTX2 | dedup law |
| If a domain file exceeds ~80KB, move correlatos to a sibling file — never split the same domain into subfiles | tree law |
| Image generation is the creative source of final map art; scripts slice, compose, validate and emit engine files — they never replace generation | tree law |
| img2threejs runs a locked staged pipeline (blockout → structure → form → material → lighting → interaction → optimization), one pass at a time; never one-shot a mesh; qualityContract before any code | 3d pipeline |
| Internals must be mechanism, not primitives; the reviewer names the symptom, not the cause, so diagnose the underlying geometry | structure |
| Lock the stage canvas before art generation; all parallax layers share the same dimensions, aspect, framing and anchor | tree law |
| lockstep capture __PUMP__(n)/__PRESENT__(n) advances exactly n frames so the frame index at the shutter is constant (fixes 10-20 frame run-to-run drift) | version lockstep |
| LOD chain 30k/10k/3k tris generated with MeshoptSimplifier LockBorder=true (naive simplification destroys UV seams and hard edges); THREE.LOD updated every 6 frames with autoUpdate=false | 3d pipeline |
| LODs 30k/10k/3k tris always generated with lockBorder so panel seams never open | 3d pipeline |
| manifest names are always new and different; content compacts into a single consolidated file when the file limit is hit | extension surface |
| Multiplayer WebRTC is co-op/casual only (every peer can lie); ~10-20% of peer pairs sit behind strict NATs; reliable/ordered channel is for events, never game-rate state (interpolate it) | structure |
| Never infer CS2 stock features from skin names: the vanilla render is the source of truth for base geometry; ask the exact market name early | structure |
| Never use Gaussian splat as physics shape — compute CANNON.Box from GLB Box3.getSize()/2; splats are visual-only photoreal layer | 3d pipeline |
| No one-shot meshes: deconstruct every item into logical components | structure |
| No flat surface: albedo variation + normal + roughness + detail at 0.5m; PBR albedo 0.02–0.9, metal 0/1, light in lux not multiplier | tree law |
| Player-visible left/right is law: A/← turns the nose/bank left on screen in chase cam; never reuse FPS strafe as vehicle steer; self-test window.__controlsTest mandatory | quality gates |
| Pointer lock requests unadjustedMovement with fallback; pitch clamps at ±89°; the viewmodel is always 3D, never a flat photo | model catalog |
| Prohibited: scattered player/ folder, central types.ts, barrels index.ts, direct node_modules imports, domain subfolders | tree law |
| Quality bar: no flat untextured surfaces, no uniform lighting, physically plausible values (albedo 0.02-0.9, metals 0/1), nothing perfectly straight/clean/repeated, every action has weight | tree law |
| R3F is vetoed: three vanilla drives the root; the physics car never sleeps (setCanSleep false) | 3d pipeline |
| React 3D rides R3F + drei + rapier: PointerLockControls is mouse-look only, the rapier character controller (capsule + autostep + snap-to-ground) replaces hand-rolled movement raycasts, and useFrame delta is always capped | windows grammar |
| Reference the showcase via env variable (IMG2THREEJS_SHOWCASE_ROOT), never an absolute path | 3d pipeline |
| Root-first flat structure: no src/, no folder inside folder; mode folders only (web/docs/tests/desktop/android/ios/cli/extension/dist) | quality gates |
| Scripts enforce structure and package evidence; they never score visuals — acceptance always comes from the agent's own vision | structure |
| Solid albedo for flat paint; never bake lighting into albedo; de-light the photo before treating it as albedo (hard requirement) | tree law |
| Stage ordering is conditional: structure → geometry → appearance, where appearance is conditioned on final geometry and never compensates for it | structure |
| StealHead multiplayer: ws join/state at 12 Hz with 100 ms interpolation and offline bot fallback | structure |
| Stealthhead locked stack: three r185 WebGL2, vanilla ESM + pinned jsDelivr importmap, zero build | stealth |
| Strict-quality blocks codegen when the spec could describe many different objects; lower the bar only on explicit user acceptance | structure |
| Surface detail is not a part: the explode contract only ships structural components as separate parts | 3d pipeline |
| Test results go in .md/lock inside the docs structure, never outside it | quality gates |
| Test scripts in docs/StealHead/mjs, test PNGs in docs/StealHead/png, TS logics at root | quality gates |
| The verdict aggregates worst patch to worst component to worst view — never a global average; a VLM never overrides a geometry hard gate | dependencies |
| Vitest specs are mandatory for pure DOM-free modules (physics, weapons, ai, state, terrain); zero test files is a P0 gap | quality gates |
| Wave and enemy stats live as configuration data, never as code; tune the economy so the player is always a little short — the shortage tension is the fun | structure |
| One shared THREE.Skeleton serves every skinned mesh on the character rig; bind in rest pose before animation; skinIndex/skinWeight stay aligned with vertex count; bounds recompute after bone motion | design system |
| 2D game art: generate2dsprite sheets use solid #FF00FF magenta chroma key (required by the processor — never invent another keyable color); abstract/geometric games (tetris/snake/pong) render procedurally — never invoke image generation for them; scene hooks are metadata only — sprites generate separately via $generate2dsprite; side-scroll stages require the stage-reference PNG before the props pipeline continues; every game ships a custom public/og.jpg + "card":"custom" + "type":"x:game" + x-banner.jpg (50:11, 1200×264) | design system |
| three.js loop law: drive with setAnimationLoop, scale all movement by delta, cap delta after background tabs, fixed-timestep accumulator for gameplay; PointerLockControls only does mouse-look (WASD is hand-written); camera forward is local -Z (the classic gotcha); dispose geometries/materials/textures manually — three.js does not GC them; target <100 draw calls | 3d pipeline |
| three r186 removed PCFSoftShadowMap — use PCFShadowMap/VSMShadowMap; keep WebGLRenderer as base and WebGPU as progressive enhancement | design system |
| 60fps budget: <120 draw calls/frame, <8ms JS, <6ms GPU, <2ms physics; car = 1 GLB ~30k tris; shadows PCFSoft 1024 not 2048 | design system |
| ADR-005: 200-300 related logics per theme file at the root with `// === SEÇÃO ===` banners; no barrels/index.ts, no src/; above ~80KB move correlatos to a sibling file without splitting the domain | design system · ADR-005 |
| Car GLB must carry POSITION+NORMAL+TANGENT and named hierarchy; never OBJ/STL (loses pivots and shadows) | design system |
| Character animation rules: player-visible left/right is law (A = nose/bank left on screen); never reuse FPS strafe as vehicle steer; inverted controls never ship — verify signs even without playing end-to-end | design |
| Chess defaults to chess.js + a board UI library, never hand-rolled rules; explicit turn FSM with one currentPlayer truth; only legal moves offered; stalemate is a draw; minimax + alpha-beta in a Web Worker; Fisher-Yates shuffle | engine law |
| Color maps use sRGB; roughness/metalness/normal/AO maps get no color-space transform | design system |
| computeVertexNormals() runs once offline, never in the animation loop | design |
| Create/resume the AudioContext synchronously inside the first real user-gesture handler (tap-to-start gate) and re-resume on visibilitychange/focus — a re-suspended context is the #1 mobile no-audio bug | audio engine |
| CSS transitions (interruptible, retargeting) for interactive state changes; keyframe animations reserved for one-shot sequences only | 3d pipeline |
| Editor stays invisible until F2 or ?edit=1 with a live ~10Hz readout of world position and orb physics numbers (radius, height, center-y) | design system |
| Enter animations split-and-stagger semantic groups (~100ms, words ~80ms) with opacity+blur+translateY; exits are subtler and shorter than enters (small fixed −12px, ~150ms) — never dramatic, never removed | language |
| Every albedo is measured, not guessed; metalness is 0 or 1 (never half-metal); diffuse dominates (L 70+) before wear has contrast | radical honesty |
| Every model ships explodable AND clickable as a structure gate: every mesh is named, explode and select share one definition of "part", and parts separate by scaling the layout — never by pushing everything the same distance | model catalog |
| Game loop rides RAF/setAnimationLoop with a single capped delta (0.05-0.1) and fixed-timestep physics with render interpolation; never setInterval/Date.now | physics |
| Games (only games) ship a second X feed card public/x-banner.jpg at exactly 50:11 (1200×264) emitted as x:game:image; never invent x:game:og, never overload og:image, never add it to __root.tsx; the safe lockup stays inside the left-most 50% × top-most 80% | design system |
| Geometry errors are reported with physical part names; texture/color errors with CS2 skin terms (pattern seed, float, finish) | design system |
| Geometry fidelity errors are named with physical part vocabulary; texture errors with CS2 skin terminology; bare-metal accents (edge, pivots, bolts) are never painted | design system |
| Glass uses transmission with opacity 1, never low opacity | design system |
| img2threejs evaluation render stays composer-free — bloom blows highlights and steals hue from candy/anodized coats; presentation post-fx only on the hero render | design system |
| Keyable animation background is always #FF00FF (chroma); loop sequences must flow end→start; motion is verified with a flip test before shipping frames | design system |
| Light-slot rule: keep lights visible and animate intensity→0 instead of toggling visible (1 light crossing frustum = +33 shader programs / 900ms stall) | design |
| Lowercase filenames, no underscore/hyphen; JSDoc in English; no emoji; 44px touch targets; grouped logic max 20 per file | writing style |
| Never cheat albedo — cheat light: viewmodel brightness is fixed with a calibrated separate light rig, not by hacking material colors | model catalog |
| Never classify a material from color alone — light-response evidence is the discriminator | design system |
| Never project the raw 2D inventory icon as albedo (flat-lit render with baked lighting is banned) | design system |
| Never ship a generated UI mock instead of the running app — a still frame is not a game | 3d pipeline |
| Never transition: all — name the exact animated properties | design |

| The database comes first, one database per callsign, with the fixed roster of maps, weapons, characters, modes and vehicles | database |
| The hijacked globalThis automation API must stay stable (AGENTS.md); getState never returns cyclic geometry/textures/objects | data |
| The in-tab wasm stays within ~320KB gz (manifold 280 + meshopt 12 + draco 30), fetched once and cached via the Cache API | 3d pipeline |
| The master game DB stays untouched in any scenario; tests run only with a real alphanumeric callsign (ghost) through real traffic, never "player" | database |
| The owner's real user DB is sacrosanct — never touched in any scenario; tests use one fixed test callsign (never the "player" ghost user) | database |
| The shading node graph serializes to JSON (texture, triplanar, noise, mix, output nodes) and deserializes to TSL at runtime — the same graph ships to web and desktop | data |
| The stealthhead validation runs the stepped protocol with the single validation record as the one truth and the verification bypass forbidden. | stealth |

| Use the better-sqlite3 native transaction wrapper — never explicit BEGIN over autocommit prepares | database |
| Username = DB name (login callsign becomes <callsign>.db); no "sandbox" prefix; alphanumeric, never numeric/uuid | sandbox isolation |
| Username equals database name: the callsign typed at login becomes <callsign>.db, one DB per user attached to the master stealthhead.db | stealth |
| vGPU/vCPU law: stealthhead.db is the virtual GPU+CPU — all processing is dispatched through adapter+orchestrator into the DB, precompiled by the owner's warm-run | stealth |

| Voxel rules: one merged mesh per chunk, never one mesh per block; mandatory face culling (across chunk borders) + greedy meshing (0fps algorithm) for scale; texture atlas = one draw call per chunk; rebuild only on edit; stream/dispose by distance; mesh+generate in Web Workers; block picking via grid-DDA voxel traversal (Amanatides & Woo) tracking the entry face — place at hitCell + face normal, reject occupied/player-overlapping cells | streaming |
| Two instruments disagreeing is information — a new metric never overrides a regression in a trusted one | audio engine |

| Grid-puzzle engine: the board is a 2D array and the view is a thin renderer — never store state in sprites/DOM; 7-bag randomizer is mandatory (pure random feels bad); SRS rotation uses the exact 5-offset wall-kick tables (y-up tables must negate y for row-down grids; I-piece has its own table; O never kicks); lock delay ~0.5s capped at ~15 resets; gravity uses delta/real time, never per-frame | model catalog |
| _headers security: X-Frame-Options DENY, nosniff, no-referrer, HSTS preload, noindex/no-store for .ts/.tsx/configs and ~40 internal dirs | 3d pipeline |

| Only knife subtypes are authorized in the finish track; other families return unsupported-family before finish selection | security |

| No Netlify Functions and no Vercel Functions — JS base, Socket realtime, static _headers/_redirects only | deploy |

| Pin exact CDN library versions on jsDelivr for deterministic reproduction; script order: es-module-shims → importmap → module | version lockstep |

| The game theme registry exposes six pure factories (terrain, object, texture, physics, controls, camera); applytheme disposes the old theme and recreates all six without touching the fixed skeleton | release gates |
| The pinned single-HTML stack is three@0.160.0 + cannon-es@0.20.0 + es-module-shims@1.8.0 via jsDelivr, exact versions never ^; shims load async before the importmap; test through a local HTTP server, never file:// | version lockstep |
| The StealHead plan gates every phase; frame-a-frame inspection runs through Playwright with the Brave executablePath capturing console/pageerror and boot screenshots (10%/50%/menu/lobby/deploy/playing) with ZERO console errors required | browser automation |
| The Vercel deploy contract: npm run build must succeed under production/SSR (dev-only deps, import-time Node APIs, runtime filesystem writes and hardcoded ports are bugs); a blank "MIME type text/html" deploy means asset base-path fallback is shadowing real assets | deploy |
| The vGPU rule: stealthhead.db pre-compiles everything (shaders, scenes, assets, music) so the user's browser never pays the cost | stealth |

## argan (103)

the dns and network application: the published library, dnssec, the custom tld verdicts and the cluster gossip.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| .alt (RFC 9476) is the free official path: your own namespace under it costs nothing and needs no ICANN | doctrine |
| Cluster middleware rejects any /cluster/* request that lacks the *.space-z.ai Origin header, a valid HMAC or a timestamp within 60 seconds; /cluster/health alone stays public | doctrine |
| DNS on a residential IP is legal in Brazil under Marco Civil; a public IP is required for the NS otherwise tunnel to a friend or VPS | dns |
| DNS research read rule: verify answers with 30-40 independent web searches before trusting them; everything must run self-hosted on the owner's own machine | dns |
| DoH via IP literal (https://47.239.134.228) self-bootstraps the resolver with no DNS at all; the git repo itself can be the zone source of truth — cloning propagates it | dns |
| EDNS advertises a 1232-byte UDP size against fragmentation on typical MTUs | doctrine |
| Elliptic curves are mandatory on the local host (RSA blows CPU/bandwidth/MTU with amplification) | doctrine |
| GitHub FOSS airdrop cutoff: accounts created after 2019-02-04 (owner 2023-04-27) are ineligible for Handshake HNS — free HNS is never guaranteed, faucets mostly dead | accounts |
| Hard verdict: free + own raw TLD + zero-config + global resolution cannot coexist — relax "global" or adopt a fixed pseudo-TLD | dns |
| Keep-alive is internal: a setInterval singleton touched on first request (pings every 5 min, AXFR sync every 30 min), never external cron | dns |
| Never operate an open resolver (DDoS amplification) | dns |
| Register both the Unicode and the Punycode form of every IDN domain | doctrine |
| Split horizon: internal-network queries answer with local IPs, external queries with the public IP (rinfo.address check in the handler) | doctrine |
| The .devthink namespace propagates through 10 channels (jsDelivr, Tor onion, Hypercore, BitTorrent DHT BEP44, Nostr, IPFS, git mirrors, PublicFreeSuffix PR, MV3 extension, unbound) | extension surface |
| The server wakes on the first request and stays alive via internal scheduled timers | doctrine |
| The sites harvest methodology: multilingual hunts (30+ languages, 1000+ terms) with an explicit blacklist — never cite or return blacklisted options, always hunt distinct alternatives | doctrine |
| Validate AI answers with targeted question loops: re-ask the 10 questions with all corrections embedded, grade each answer (correct/wrong/vague/incomplete), and track the score improving across rounds | doctrine |
| ~30 grouped logics in one file covering one whole category/topic is the ideal file density (owner preference) | tree law |
| A custom root zone only resolves globally if other recursors adopt it — unilateral ICANN bypass is impossible | dns |
| Argan routes live in src/app/ (not src/app/api/) with max 2 folder levels inside src/ | tree law |
| Argan sovereignty verdict: raw TLD + zero-config + global + R$0 are mutually exclusive; trust anchors go out-of-band (RFC 7958); real DNSSEC from day one is the moat | dns |
| CNAME never lives at the apex; use ALIAS/ANAME flattening for apex aliasing | structure |
| dns operational scripts live under tests/ and downloaded binaries under tests/bin — never a scripts/ folder | dns |
| DoH over port 443 is the only path when the ISP blocks port 53 — an always-on free VPS relays to the dynamic home IP | dns |
| Naming without ICANN is the standard, not the exception: ~600 naming systems mapped in four modes (central agency, consortium-standard, ledger, math/key) — the custom TLD may use any or all modes together | dns |
| Non-official TLDs are proven with Google Dorking step-by-step checklists before accepting shallow AI answers; a name looking like a TLD does not mean root delegation | wave method |
| soochimp robots.txt blocks all AI-training/scraping bots (GPTBot, CCBot, anthropic-ai, ClaudeBot, ChatGPT-User, Google-Extended, Bytespider) while allowing Googlebot/Bingbot; devthink.tink instead allows every AI bot — AI-bot policy is a per-app decision | cli craft |
| The DNS delegation truth: a DNS server can serve any TLD immediately, but other resolvers only use your root server if manually configured as root hints — there is no protocol that announces a root zone | wave method |
| The root zone is "." with SOA/NS plus delegation, never ".devthink" directly | wave method |
| Windows firewall order: the allow rule comes first and the block rule after, because the first matching rule wins and block-first blocks everything | windows grammar |
| WireGuard builds private tunnels and never exposes services (ListenPort 51820, not 53); Windows firewall is first-matching-rule-wins — ALLOW UDP 53 first, then BLOCK TCP 53 AXFR | dns |
| Z.ai sandbox restrictions honored: port 53 blocked → DoH only; cron forbidden as scheduler (use setInterval); Next output standalone mandatory; src flat max 2 levels | sandbox isolation |
| Argan: DNS wire protocol handwritten in TS with no DNS libraries; DNSSEC KSK+ZSK mandatory on *.devthink.pro; DoT port 853, DoH port 443; ACME DNS-01 wildcard certificates | dns |
| DNSSEC trust-anchor bootstrap is out-of-band by design (RFC 7958); a resolver cannot learn to trust a new root via DNS itself; factory resolvers hardcode the 13 root servers and the root KSK | dns |
| Sovereignty is opt in by design; real dnssec day one | dns |
| Sovereignty is opt in by design: the worldwide without installing lives in the pendurado, the alternative lives in the installed, the gateway covers who cannot touch the resolver | dns |
| Windows-DNS stack: mkcert wildcard certs (Firefox 100+ imports OS roots); CGNAT detected via 100.64.x.x public IP → needs VPS/friend for inbound; root zone not needed — just the TLD zone + secondary | dns |
| Zero hardcode in the DNS lib: everything via environment with explicit error when a mandatory var is missing | dns |
| A real custom tld needs icann approval (185k usd, 2026 round open until august 12); use subdomains, and never .local which is reserved for mdns (rfc 6762), use .internal, .home, .lan or .test | dns |
| AI-answer verification loop: re-ask the source 10 questions that embed the already-corrected errors inside the question text, then verify every claim with 40-70 web searches before trusting it | code |
| CGNAT ranges detected from the real public IP decide a reverse tunnel or a friend-VPN peer; home port-forward never rides CGNAT | ports |
| Cloudflare Secondary DNS (AXFR zone transfers) is Enterprise-plan only — free/pro support primary or CNAME setups only; NS-Global and le_dns are the genuinely free AXFR secondary alternatives | dns |
| deSEC is the chosen free DNS host when port 53 cannot be exposed (REST API, automatic DNSSEC, EU jurisdiction); someone must own port 53 — there is no magic | dns |
| DevTink DNS secondary architecture: mname is the Node library (AXFR/IXFR + TSIG + TCP + EDNS) pulling zones from a Technitium primary; split-horizon answers internal IPs locally and public IPs externally | dns |
| DNS environment defaults: web port 3000, HTTPS 443, DNS 53, timeout 5s with retries, 8.8.8.8 default resolver, restrictive SPF, default-issuer CAA, TTL 3600 with NS/SOA 86400, annual RSA-2048/SHA-256 certificate | dns |
| DNS in sandbox runs as DoH through a Next.js API route — never port 53 | sandbox isolation |
| dns tests/ scripts stay CommonJS (.cjs) so they run with zero build step | dns |
| Hurricane Electric secondary: slave.dns.he.net (216.218.133.2) performs the axfr, so incoming tcp 53 is allowed only from that ip; he email registration is broken and cloudflare secondary dns is enterprise-only | dns |
| Never modify ZAI .zscripts — DNS ships as DoH (RFC 8484) over an API route: no port 53, no mini-services, no external cron | dns |
| Port 53 is impossible in the ZAI sandbox (zero Linux capabilities); DNS serves DoH (RFC 8484) via Caddy :81 → Next.js; never modify ZAI .sh scripts (production breaks; only build.sh BUILD_DIR line is safe); output:"standalone" must remain; ZAI cron is AI chat-based — keep-alive must be internal setInterval timers started on first request (ping every 5min, AXFR sync every 30min) | sandbox isolation |
| The argan panel works as an embedded registrar: the typed label validates, becomes a real subdomain under the purchased apex, and serves through the gateway immediately with a public URL | code |
| The custom tld is framed as self hosted and never as a free domain: paid paths excluded, handshake requirement excluded, icann path excluded. | dns |

| Atomic truncation keeps tool_calls and tool results together; compact/summarize only when needed | doctrine |

## vault (13)

the storage engine: chunked blobs, the sealed database and the faun lesson.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| Vault policies: the deny overrides the allow and every action lands in the audit row. | dependencies |
| Vault robots.txt blocks scraping, SEO-mining, AI-training and abusive download agents everywhere while advertising robots stay welcome | structure |
| Every hard disk is a database; the person opens as many as wanted and the external storages connect opt in. | database |
| Git objects are the source of truth: the repository is the disk of the platform | data |
| ids are time-ordered 64-bit bigints (zero storage, deterministic tests); the blob store contract is put(stream) → {id, bytes, sha256, kind} with the vault owning the engine and the gateway serving it, git-lfs friendly | streaming |
| The database is never readable or exportable by the stranger, only the registered people. | database |
| vault is all storage and only it; supabase and aws bucket competitor; ships security first with the faun lesson encoded | blob storage |
| Vault pointer types and git-storage strategies are cataloged — use the cataloged one, never invent | model catalog |
| Object bytes encrypt at rest aes-256-gcm under per-project wrapped keys; the master key rides env or is generated and persisted; iv of 12 bytes, never math.random | secrets |
| Vault records carry labels and digests, never plaintext values; payment/delete/credential confirmations resolve only by distinct human action; timeout never resolves; approval is never batched | consent gates |
| The decoy database serves anonymous probes; a downloadable database on a static deploy is the failure never to repeat | deploy |
| The vault chunks carry the checksum with catalog dedupe and reuse; the quotas ride the plan through chunks, never a literal fixed size. | dedup law |
| Vault monetizes through Google AdSense (ads.txt publisher declaration) | release gates |

## forge (25)

the character pipeline application: forge runs only while foundry runs and stores — twin sites of the sandbox engine, with the reconstruction gates and the evidence doctrine.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| After every pass the agent picks exactly one action: continue, refine-spec, refine-code, request-input, or stop | doctrine |
| CS2 knife gates are fixture data, not prose: silhouette IoU ≥0.85, aspect-ratio delta ≤0.05, scale delta ≤0.08, projection coverage ≥0.85, finish/material ≥0.80, painted-region ≥0.80, orbit collapse ≤0.15; a wrong family or degenerate orbit cannot be overridden by a global visual score | doctrine |
| exactness tiers: image-only / metadata-assisted / exact-texture — the skin name alone never earns exact-texture; albedo is never reused as roughness, normal, AO or metalness; a good global score never overrides a failed critical feature | dependencies |
| external reference tools may produce evidence and diagnostics only — never silently provide meshes, decide hidden geometry, mutate the accepted source, or approve a pass | doctrine |
| the reference view and the meaningful orbit views remain separate gates | doctrine |
| Valve pixels are never committed until provenance is verified; cs2_textures/ stays outside the repository as the IP boundary | radical honesty |
| every detail entry must mapsTo a real component.localFeatures or material.localOverrides entry — a prose-only detail is a gate failure; targetMinDetails is 3/6/10/16 by complexity tier | dependencies |
| Pass order is fixed and locked: blockout → structural → form-refinement → material → surface → lighting → interaction → optimization; a pass unlocks only with a real render + comparison sheet + vision score at threshold | structure |
| the character track builds a stylized bust, never a face-copy, with a filled anatomy block (head-units, proportions, face landmarks) | structure |
| L-Void subtraction enters the SDF before meshing, never after; L3 rigid parts get forced weight 1.0 overriding the falloff; L5 markings mix in shader from a spatial function, never vertex colors on marching-cubes meshes | design system |
| depth model output is a relative prior only — never metric dimensions, never invented backsides | radical honesty |
| Every spec part traces to an evidenceRef in an admitted reference view — a part that cannot is deleted, not guessed (a generator that emits a mane or scales the reference lacks is hallucinating) | code |
| never accept an MCP-only scene edit; copy the proven parameter change into the spec or generated TypeScript, rebuild and recapture | code |
| glow details record hotspot position relative to the key light direction — a gloss with no matching light direction will not render visibly; fasteners are always instanced systems, never one-off meshes; engraved lines are real geometry grooves while painted lines are decals and panel-lines are AO seams | data |
| local spec search is a mandatory pipeline stage (BM25 over cs2/core_3d collections, evidence bundle with source_refs): never invent domain specs when local evidence exists; preserve JSONL records and provenance rather than cache output | radical honesty |
| Every part of a character maps to exactly one primary geometry layer (L0 core, L1 appendage, L2 cavity, L3 single-bone isolate, L4 cross-joint shell) plus modifier layers (L-1 scaffold, L-Void, L5 markings, L-Proxy); the classification test is "does this part cross a joint?" | stealth |
| Playwright routes use isolated headless 1600x900 profiles with service workers blocked; Firefox/WebKit run only to validate a cross-browser discrepancy, never all three every cycle | sandbox isolation |
| SAM2 masks use one positive point inside the visible subject; separate masks per identity system (body, wing, blade, hair, face) — a global foreground mask is never component decomposition | security |
| Scripts enforce, the model judges: deterministic Python (stdlib-only, no pip/PIL/numpy) handles validation/gating/packaging; model tokens go only to looking at one side-by-side sheet and deciding pass/fail | secrets |
| the correction loop is bounded (success/repeated-defect/oscillation/plateau/hard-ceiling) and escalates to request-input — never a silent infinite token burn | secrets |
| Context7 answers are version-pinned to the installed Three.js; local lockfile, .d.ts and typecheck override live documentation; WebGPU/TSL advice never applies to the WebGL path without an explicit version migration | version lockstep |
| Forge and foundry are never new apps: roles of the sandbox engine; the library publishes, the clone never distributes | sandbox isolation |
| forge runs only, foundry runs and stores; deployable site clones from saddle, no distribution, exist only as sites | deploy |
| Noise-derived normalMap/bumpMap forbidden on smooth stylized skin: roughness 0.5-0.6, metalness 0, clearcoat 0.15, sheen 1.0 | version lockstep |
| robots.txt: no domain hardcoded (binds on the deploy platform); SEO bots and AI training crawlers blocked outright; .ts/.tsx/.json disallowed | deploy |

## cobaia (8)

the monetization site of the family: honest ad slots, the plans page and the ethical limit.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| Community validation always carries numbers + region + stack; without a dashboard print it is guesswork; offerwall A/B decides in 14 days on revenue per 1000 weekly actives | doctrine |
| Keyword research is systematic: 10 verbs × 6 objects × 8 formats × 6 intents matrix (~2880 slots, 1320-2640 unique after dedup) + official ad glossaries, revalidated with keyword planners | dedup law |
| The monetization rides cobaia with the ethical limit absolute: invisible click mirroring, hidden ad iframes and bot traffic are invalid traffic and never enter the tree; ad slots stay honest and empty until approval | 3d pipeline |
| The checkout answers the not configured state honestly, stores no card data, and the payment identity, account and network register together to block duplication | design system |
| The cobaia checkout answers the not configured state honestly; no card data is stored and the traffic is the human traffic only. | design system |
| Cobaia is the family monetization surface: honest ad slots, empty-state checkout links and support rails keep the family sites clean while plans carry revenue | cli craft |
| Monetization follows the interaction matrix: content → display+affiliate+metered; game/H5 → rewarded+offerwall+shortlink; tool/API → metering+premium keys+sponsors; community → job board+directory+sponsored+membership | secrets |
| The monetization lives in the cobaia site: real ad slots, honest referral, plans, support and blog; the family sites stay clean | code |

## cli (342)

the terminal and the qz plugin: random ports, no defaults, the zai cookie bridge and the headless doctrine.

| rule | detail |
| --- | --- |
| One name, one meaning: a name with two semantics is renamed so each semantic exists exactly once | doctrine |
| 16 subagents read docs and talks while 16 more read the current logics, then the orchestrator cross-compares for full implementation | wave method |
| A new CLI conversation opens a new chat ID; never loop in the same chat ID | cli craft |
| Anti-loop: never resend the AI's own response as if it were the user's prompt | doctrine |
| autoupdate:false is permanent in every config generation: auto-update must never swap the config under the agent | doctrine |
| Canonical precedence for divergent duplicates: live-validated variant > most complete superset > domain-owner variant, documented in JSDoc | precedence |
| canOpenBrowser is false in ALL modes; browser always internal Playwright headless, never CDP/external spawn | doctrine |
| CLI Z.ai↔OpenCode sends only the last message (no history) | cli craft |
| Conversation identity is the /c/{uuid} URL path captured after the first message submit | doctrine |
| Cross-platform: PowerShell, CLI, CMD, browser, desktop; no platform assumptions | cli craft |
| CUSTOM_LOGO: string[] = [] empty slot lets the user paste ASCII art via Ctrl+V; logo centered + text box below; no top bar | doctrine |
| Degenerate answers ("Yes"/"Ok") are detected and redone once with a corrective directive | doctrine |
| Env vars carry module prefixes (DEVTHINK_*, QZ_*, WEBSCRAPE_*, VIDEO_*); config precedence is env > file > shared defaults | precedence |
| Every duplicated logic family merges into exactly ONE owner file; no file repeats a configuration, constant or name another file owns | tree law |
| GLM-5.3 thinking cannot be disabled: requests carrying thinking.type "disabled" must switch to "enabled" before adopting glm-5.3 or the request fails; pin reasoning_effort low for research workloads | streaming |
| Ink v7 + React 19.2.6 works via react-reconciler v0.33 — never downgrade | cli craft |
| Interactivity must be natural — commands are accepted with or without the slash/hyphen prefix | doctrine |
| Internal mapped memory (Map) is the primary store; external file persistence is the fallback only — both fully interchangeable | memory tiers |
| Internal memory is always preferred and external filesystem is fallback — and a fallback must actually load back (write-only persistence is a defect) | memory tiers |
| Local training: SSM state tuning (per-user state, zero inference overhead, ~1MB/user) after each session >5min; training data stays local, never sent to external servers, user opt-out | doctrine |
| Max 2-3 steps to any action; no vim modes; arrow-key navigation only; every action gives immediate visual feedback; Inquirer suspends Ink for selections | cli craft |
| Memory read priority session → project → global (external opt-in only); write: summary→session, significant→project, preference→global; never auto-write external | memory tiers |
| Modo somente-max: collapse variants to the maximum step | operation ladder · F-DTK-769 |
| Never auto-send at startup — no message leaves until the user or an explicit job requests it | doctrine |
| Never monkey-patch the global fetch — inject a ProxiedFetch class; restore the original on cleanup | doctrine |
| No lying stubs — an endpoint returning empty arrays or fake connectivity is a defect to fix or remove | doctrine |
| No message is sent until the user interacts; without an @ prefix send only the literal text — instruction injection requires an explicit prefix | doctrine |
| No side effects at module load: config and session systems initialize lazily on first call | doctrine |
| Parallel edits with identical oldString fail silently — re-verify every section after batch edits and re-edit what dropped | doctrine |
| Plan mode automatically carries edit:deny and bash:deny | doctrine |
| Qwen thinking_summary is cumulative — replace the previous summary, never concatenate it | cli craft |
| qz.exe double-click shows full help + "press ENTER" pause only when interactive (stdin.isTTY); pipe/args mode exits without pause; pipe detection uses isTTY === false (strict) | cli craft |
| qz is a pure CLI: no custom interactive loop — the terminal itself (PowerShell/cmd) is the interactive mode; no args shows help and exits | cli craft |
| RSS dedup rides SHA-256 of normalized text + feed GUID + canonicalized URL fingerprint; semantic dedup only above 0.95 embedding similarity | dedup law |
| RSS ingest health thresholds: duplication rate <5%, feed→ingest latency <30s, active-feed coverage >95%; a feed down for >3 polls alerts, 5xx five times goes critical | doctrine |
| RSS pipeline: dedup phase 1 = canonical URL + SHA256, phase 2 = embedding similarity; analytics targets refresh >10 items/h, duplication <5%, latency <30s, coverage >95% | dedup law |
| RWKV-7 is the SSM of choice for agentic state tracking (spike-free training, mature ecosystem); pure SSMs close the associative-recall gap only with a little attention | privacy |
| Send only the last user message — never the full history; context is kept via the chat_id | doctrine |
| Stealth mode is mandatory — sites must not detect the automation | stealth |
| Subagents abort frequently in general mode — do the work directly instead of delegating | wave method |
| Switch mode before sending the message; never reactivate a mode that is already active | doctrine |
| The binary works standalone with every feature embedded and no external deps | doctrine |
| The CLI never hangs when stdin is not a TTY: readStdin 3s timeout, pauseIfTTY 5s, login browser launch timeout 30s | cli craft |
| The fingerprint stays minimal: only the user-agent on content requests; the device id header stays removed to minimize rate limiting | stealth |
| The house default reasoning effort is max, never "high" | doctrine |
| The user reverts previous work — always confirm the current state before proceeding | doctrine |
| The zai plugin drives the stealth Brave headless — it never opens the user's visible browser | browser automation |
| The zai plugin prompt text works only in chat mode; in agent mode nothing is sent because the agent already knows how to act, and every finished command gives open code cli feedback | cli craft |
| Tool results flow back through the same path as the first message (into the same text area) | doctrine |
| All ports of all logic are drawn randomly (mathematical lottery, e.g. 1024–65535 / up to 1M), locked once chosen; no fixed port (3000/3001/21000) anywhere; localhost only, never hostname; tests boot the dev server and capture its random port | ports |
| Atomic writes: tmp+rename, chmod 600 (dir 0700), stale-lock detection, Node-builtin validation | structure |
| canOpenBrowser stays false in all 9 runtime modes — never open an external browser; use Playwright's bundled Chromium (avoids "browser already running" profile locks), PROFILE_DIR ~/.config/opencode/qz/playwright | qz plugin |
| CDP connect first to existing Brave (http://127.0.0.1:9222); else launch headless with separate zai-headless-profile — never lock the user's main Brave profile | browser automation |
| Collision fixes rename instead of deleting (_alt/_Legacy/_Local suffixes) and edit only existing files — no new modular files during cleanup | physics |
| Config precedence: full OpenCode config loads as the fallback layer under the full DevThink config; flags > env > config | precedence |
| Duplicates in merged files are preserved by renaming with _legacy/_alt/Local suffixes — never deleted when the owner forbids removal | dedup law |

| OpenCode auth reads from ~/.local/share/opencode (never ~/.config/opencode) | qz plugin |
| Pairing: 8-char code, SHA-256 digest, 5-minute expiry, short bearer token in sessionStorage; browser never receives credentials | secrets |
| Pairing contract: provider API keys and OAuth refresh data are never returned to the browser; the raw one-time code is consumed and only a short-lived token remains | secrets |
| Permission schema is allow/ask/deny with glob patterns; "*": "ask" is the safe default | security |
| persistAuth() writes to 3 destinations in sequence: storage-state.json, auth.json (always), opencode.json auth key (if OpenCode present); auth.json saves in BOTH plugin dir and ~/.local/share/opencode/auth.json to avoid conflicts | blob storage |

| Provider credentials enter only through explicit commands or env; never browser cookie stores, website passwords, or web-session cookies; conversation records carry no credential values | secrets |

| Provider login is user-owned: official authorization URL or documented API key only — the CLI core never extracts browser cookies or requests passwords for website automation | secrets |
| Provider-neutral boundary: no browser cookie capture, no third-party session automation, no CAPTCHA bypass, no forged device signals, no undocumented endpoints; credentials always belong to the user | secrets |
| Provider tokens, auth records, server lifecycle control and filesystem level operations remain local cli only capabilities | secrets |
| qz auth is saved to both locations at once (plugin auth.json and the OpenCode auth.json) so the two stores never conflict | qz plugin |

| qz works standalone AND as an OpenCode plugin; auth is cookie capture, never API keys — one capture authenticates every mode; persistAuth writes 3 destinations (storage-state.json, auth.json always, opencode.json when present) | secrets |

| RSS XML parsing runs with DTD/XXE disabled, SSRF-guarded URL allowlists, HTTP timeouts, HTML sanitization before render, and per-feed rate limiting | security |
| RWKV conversation-state compression: the recurrent state (~256KB, constant) replaces the full message history — keep only the last ~128 tokens in the buffer plus an optional summary; models lazy-download (~90MB INT4) into ~/.devthink/models on first run | secrets |
| Simple names without hyphens (auth.ts, not auth-sdk.ts) | security |
| Supported auth only: API keys, bearer, documented provider HTTP auth via env/config; credentials redacted from status output, exports and errors | secrets |
| Test scripts never overwrite auth.json in test mode; multi-account capture rotates via flat zai-1..zai-5 keys and the browser closes only after the SSE stream completes (safeclose + 60s backup timeout, never a fixed 3s timer) | secrets |
| The auth data lives in the isolated short lowercase folder, zipped at level 9 maximum | writing style |
| The auth store encrypts with aes-256-gcm under a master key from env, then key file, then scrypt of hostname plus username; auth.json rides 0600 permissions with a 30s cache and multi-account rotation | model catalog |
| The browser never accepts provider secrets; the credential store is empty by default and outside the sync model | secrets |
| The browser stores the pairing token only in session storage; every browser request is origin checked, bearer authenticated and content type checked for json mutation bodies | secrets |
| The CLI binary has no default mode, model or token: the user defines everything; internal mapped memory always, external memory only as fallback; at most 20 types or 20 features per file; no hyphenated names; the host port is drawn between 0 and one million and localhost never exists | secrets |
| The CLI root files carry category prefixes (agent-, auth-, cli-, core-, db-, memory-, plugin-, sdk-, server-, shell-, tui-, ui-, themes-, markdown-, side-) with hyphen separators, one re-export barrel per category, and devthink.ts stays the never-touched entry point | database |
| The config copies the vendor exactly: the real context the endpoint honors and never the marketing one, the official path capitalization, the exact token limits, the official id, and the output never exceeding the context | secrets |
| The oauth callback server locks to 127.0.0.1 loopback, never 0.0.0.0; the accounts file writes atomically (tmp plus rename) with chmod 600 | ports |

| The QZ plugin solves the Baxia sliding captcha inside its iframe with retries and a background watcher | captcha policy |

| Banner under 10 lines with version shown, toggleable (--no-banner), skipped in CI and screen-reader mode, tested at 80 columns | version lockstep |
